CUSTOM HOUSE, NEWCHWANG, 15TH OcToB1 1902. SIR, I.-The Imrial Russian Provisional Civil Administration having opned, on the 12th August i900, a Custom House to collect, under Foreign supervision, Dues and Likin on Junk rgoes, I now transmit the statistic of that trade from ist July to 3oth September 1902. On all cargoes arriving or leaving by sea-going junks Dues and Duties were collected at Treaty Tariff rates and Likin at the rate of 1 per cent. ad valorem on Imports and 100 per cent. ad valorer on Exports, with the exceptio of the Duties on certain specified staples, which were reduced on ist May 190r to the rate formerly assessed by tu Native Customs. Boats trading up river.paid Tonnage Dues, but no Duties were levied on their cargoes. II. TONNAGE.--The number and movements of sea-going junks, inwards and outwards, during the quarter, are set forth in the following Table:- JULY. AUGUST. SEPTEMBR. TOTAL. No. Piculs. No. Piculs. In conformity with the instructions contained in your Circular No. 381, Second Series, I now transmit t the Statistical Department at Shanghai, for publication, the statistics of the trade at Kowloon and the neighbouring stationst Native vessels for the third quarter of the year 19o2. b. 1902.] KIAOCHO0W. 255 (Received 30h October 1902.) -CUSTOM HOUSE, KIAOCHOW, 15TH OCTOBER 1902. SIR, I.-a. In conformity &#39;with the instructions contained in your Circulars Nos. 2i of 1869, 14 of 1872, and 6 of 1875, I now transmit to the Statistical Department at Shanghai, for publication, the statistics of the trade of this port for the third quarter of the year 1902. b. The Custom House ( 1 I) was opened on the ist July 1899. The collection of Tariff Import ana Export Duty and Convention Likin on Opium (Hk.yh 30 + 80 per picul), and the collection of Native Duties and Likin on Junk Cargoes at half Tariff rates, commenced on that day. The stations at present provisionally controlled by the Kiaochow Customs are known as Tsangkou ( 0); Nuku ( ); Shatszkou ($ ), including Tengyao ( ); Hungshihyai (f ; ); Taputou ( A 1 ); and Lingshanwei ( W ), including Tachiangkou (* U). c. The word &quot;Inwards&quot; in these statistics means to China, and the word &quot;Outwards,&quot; from China, th statistics of goods landed and consumed in German territory not being included.
